{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_62","type":"reasoning","text":"**Confirming injection bypass cause and patch plan**\n\nThe vulnerability likely arises because `@JacksonInject` with an empty string id defaults to `useInput = true`, letting JSON input override injection when no injectable values are set on the ObjectMapper used (as in the sampler). The fix is to update all `@JacksonInject JavaScriptConfig` annotations to explicitly set `useInput = OptBoolean.FALSE` across all related classes to block JSON overriding injection."}}
